```
30	    """Strip every KV_SECRET_* env var so tests start from a clean slate."""
31	    import os

33	    for key in list(os.environ):
34	        if key.startswith("KV_SECRET_"):
35	            monkeypatch.delenv(key, raising=False)

38	# ── DB-backed test support ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
39	# DB integration tests require a real Postgres (the models use JSONB / UUID /
40	# gen_random_uuid(), which SQLite can't host). Set TEST_DATABASE_URL to enable
41	# them; without it the db_session fixture skips, so `pytest` still runs the
42	# pure-unit suite anywhere. CI provides an ephemeral Postgres service.
43	
44	TEST_DATABASE_URL = os.environ.get("TEST_DATABASE_URL")
45	
46	
47	@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
48	def _db_engine() -> "Iterator[object]":
49	    from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
50	
51	    import backend.app.db.models  # noqa: F401 — registers tables on Base.metadata
52	    from backend.app.db.base import Base
53	
54	    if not TEST_DATABASE_URL:
55	        pytest.skip("TEST_DATABASE_URL not set; skipping DB-backed tests")
56	
57	    engine = create_engine(TEST_DATABASE_URL, future=True)
58	    try:
59	        with engine.connect() as conn:
60	            conn.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
61	    except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - environment-dependent
62	        engine.dispose()
63	        pytest.skip(f"TEST_DATABASE_URL not reachable: {TEST_DATABASE_URL}")
64	
65	    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
66	    yield engine
67	    Base.metadata.drop_all(engine)
68	    engine.dispose()
69	
70	
71	@pytest.fixture
72	def db_session(_db_engine: object) -> "Iterator[object]":
73	    """A transactional Session rolled back after each test for isolation.
74	
75	    join_transaction_mode="create_savepoint" lets code under test call
76	    commit() freely — those commits land on a savepoint inside the outer
77	    transaction, which is rolled back here, so tests never persist.
78	    """
79	    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session as SASession
80	
81	    connection = _db_engine.connect()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
82	    trans = connection.begin()
83	    session = SASession(bind=connection, join_transaction_mode="create_savepoint")
84	    try:
85	        yield session
86	    finally:
87	        session.close()
88	        trans.rollback()
89	        connection.close()
90
```

```
1	"""Probe endpoint tests against a real Postgres (db_session) via TestClient.
2	
3	get_db is overridden to the test session so requests share the rolled-back
4	transaction; run_suite.delay is spied so no broker is needed. Auth runs in
5	dev-bypass mode (conftest), which upserts the dev user into the same session.
6	"""
7	
8	import uuid
9	from collections.abc import Iterator
10	from typing import Any
11	
12	import pytest
13	from fastapi import HTTPException
14	from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
15	from sqlalchemy import select
16	
17	from backend.app.api.v1 import probe as probe_module
18	from backend.app.core.auth import get_current_user
19	from backend.app.db.models import Check, Connection, Result, Run, Suite
20	from backend.app.db.session import get_db
21	from backend.app.main import app
22	from backend.app.services.probe import PROBE_CONNECTION_NAME, PROBE_SUITE_NAME
23	
24	
25	@pytest.fixture
26	def probe_client(
27	    db_session: Any, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
28	) -> Iterator[tuple[TestClient, list[Any]]]:
29	    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db_session
30	    delay_calls: list[tuple[Any, ...]] = []
31	    monkeypatch.setattr(
32	        probe_module.run_suite, "delay", lambda *args, **_kw: delay_calls.append(args)
33	    )
34	    try:
35	        yield TestClient(app), delay_calls
36	    finally:
37	        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
38	
39	
40	# ───────────────────────── POST ────────────────────────────────────
41	
42	
43	def test_post_creates_queued_run_and_dispatches(
44	    probe_client: tuple[TestClient, list[Any]], db_session: Any
45	) -> None:
46	    client, delay_calls = probe_client
47	    resp = client.post("/api/v1/_probe/snowflake-suite")
48	
49	    assert resp.status_code == 202
50	    body = resp.json()
51	    assert body["status"] == "queued"
52	
53	    run = db_session.get(Run, uuid.UUID(body["run_id"]))
54	    assert run is not None and run.status == "queued"
55	    assert run.triggered_by.startswith("probe:")
56	
57	    # fixtures seeded
58	    assert db_session.scalars(
59	        select(Connection).where(Connection.name == PROBE_CONNECTION_NAME)
60	    ).first()
61	
62	    # dispatched once with (str(run_id), table)
63	    assert len(delay_calls) == 1
64	    assert delay_calls[0][0] == body["run_id"]
65	
66	
67	def test_post_dispatch_failure_marks_run_failed(
68	    db_session: Any, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
69	) -> None:
70	    """Broker unreachable: the run must not be left stuck 'queued'."""
71	    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db_session
72	
73	    def _boom(*_a: Any, **_k: Any) -> None:
74	        raise RuntimeError("broker down")
75	
76	    monkeypatch.setattr(probe_module.run_suite, "delay", _boom)
77	    try:
78	        resp = TestClient(app).post("/api/v1/_probe/snowflake-suite")
79	        assert resp.status_code == 503
80	        run = db_session.scalars(select(Run)).first()
81	        assert run is not None and run.status == "failed"
82	    finally:
83	        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
84	
85	
86	def test_post_is_idempotent_across_calls(
87	    probe_client: tuple[TestClient, list[Any]], db_session: Any
88	) -> None:
89	    client, _ = probe_client
90	    client.post("/api/v1/_probe/snowflake-suite")
91	    client.post("/api/v1/_probe/snowflake-suite")
92	
93	    assert (
94	        len(
95	            db_session.scalars(
96	                select(Connection).where(Connection.name == PROBE_CONNECTION_NAME)
97	            ).all()
98	        )
99	        == 1
100	    )
101	    assert len(db_session.scalars(select(Suite).where(Suite.name == PROBE_SUITE_NAME)).all()) == 1
102	    assert len(db_session.scalars(select(Check)).all()) == 1
103	    # two runs, though
104	    assert len(db_session.scalars(select(Run)).all()) == 2
105	
106	
107	# ───────────────────────── GET ─────────────────────────────────────
108	
109	
110	def test_get_returns_run_with_results(
111	    probe_client: tuple[TestClient, list[Any]], db_session: Any
112	) -> None:
113	    client, _ = probe_client
114	    run_id = client.post("/api/v1/_probe/snowflake-suite").json()["run_id"]
115	
116	    # simulate the worker having persisted a result
117	    check = db_session.scalars(select(Check)).first()
118	    db_session.add(
119	        Result(
120	            run_id=uuid.UUID(run_id),
121	            check_id=check.id,
122	            status="passed",
123	            observed_value={"observed_value": 5},
124	            expected_value={"min_value": 1},
125	        )
126	    )
127	    db_session.commit()
128	
129	    resp = client.get(f"/api/v1/_probe/runs/{run_id}")
130	    assert resp.status_code == 200
131	    body = resp.json()
132	    assert body["status"] == "queued"
133	    assert len(body["results"]) == 1
134	    assert body["results"][0]["status"] == "passed"
135	    assert body["results"][0]["observed_value"] == {"observed_value": 5}
136	
137	
138	def test_get_unknown_run_returns_404(probe_client: tuple[TestClient, list[Any]]) -> None:
139	    client, _ = probe_client
140	    resp = client.get(f"/api/v1/_probe/runs/{uuid.uuid4()}")
141	    assert resp.status_code == 404
142	
143	
144	def test_get_run_without_results_returns_empty_list(
145	    probe_client: tuple[TestClient, list[Any]],
146	) -> None:
147	    client, _ = probe_client
148	    run_id = client.post("/api/v1/_probe/snowflake-suite").json()["run_id"]
149	    # No worker ran (delay is spied), so the queued run has no results yet.
150	    resp = client.get(f"/api/v1/_probe/runs/{run_id}")
151	    assert resp.status_code == 200
152	    body = resp.json()
153	    assert body["status"] == "queued"
154	    assert body["results"] == []
155	
156	
157	def test_get_malformed_run_id_returns_422(probe_client: tuple[TestClient, list[Any]]) -> None:
158	    client, _ = probe_client
159	    resp = client.get("/api/v1/_probe/runs/not-a-uuid")
160	    assert resp.status_code == 422
161	
162	
163	# ───────────────────────── auth gating ─────────────────────────────
164	
165	
166	def test_post_requires_auth(db_session: Any) -> None:
167	    """The handler must not run (no Run created) when auth rejects the request."""
168	    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db_session
169	
170	    def _reject() -> None:
171	        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="unauthorized")
172	
173	    app.dependency_overrides[get_current_user] = _reject
174	    try:
175	        resp = TestClient(app).post("/api/v1/_probe/snowflake-suite")
176	        assert resp.status_code == 401
177	        assert db_session.scalars(select(Run)).all() == []
178	    finally:
179	        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
180
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/backend/app/api/v1/connections.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Connection CRUD + connectivity-test endpoints.\n\nThin HTTP layer over `connection_service`: validates request shapes, wires the\ncurrent user + db session + secret store, and maps models onto responses. All\nbusiness logic (validation dispatch, secret write-through, connectivity probe)\nlives in the service. Responses never carry secret material — only `has_secret`.\n\nThe `/test` route is a sync ``def`` so FastAPI runs it in a worker thread; the\nSnowflake connect is blocking and must not stall the event loop.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport uuid\nfrom typing import Annotated, Any\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, status\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field\nfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n\nfrom backend.app.core.auth import get_current_user\nfrom backend.app.core.secrets import SecretStore, get_secret_store\nfrom backend.app.db.models import Connection, User\nfrom backend.app.db.session import get_db\nfrom backend.app.services import connection_service as svc\n\nrouter = APIRouter(tags=[\"connections\"])\n\n\nclass ConnectionCreate(BaseModel):\n    name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=128)\n    type: str\n    env: str\n    config: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n    secret: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Credential; write-only, never returned\")\n\n\nclass ConnectionUpdate(BaseModel):\n    name: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=128)\n    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None\n    secret: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"Rotate the credential; write-only\")\n\n\nclass ConnectionRead(BaseModel):\n    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n\n    id: uuid.UUID\n    name: str\n    type: str\n    env: str\n    config: dict[str, Any]\n    has_secret: bool\n    created_by: uuid.UUID\n\n    @classmethod\n    def from_model(cls, conn: Connection) -> \"ConnectionRead\":\n        return cls(\n            id=conn.id,\n            name=conn.name,\n            type=conn.type,\n            env=conn.env,\n            config=dict(conn.config),\n            has_secret=conn.secret_ref is not None,\n            created_by=conn.created_by,\n        )\n\n\nclass ConnectionTestResult(BaseModel):\n    ok: bool\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/connections\",\n    response_model=ConnectionRead,\n    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,\n    summary=\"Create a connection\",\n)\ndef create_connection(\n    payload: ConnectionCreate,\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],\n    secret_store: Annotated[SecretStore, Depends(get_secret_store)],\n) -> ConnectionRead:\n    conn = svc.create_connection(\n        db,\n        name=payload.name,\n        conn_type=payload.type,\n        env=payload.env,\n        config=payload.config,\n        secret=payload.secret,\n        created_by=current_user.id,\n        secret_store=secret_store,\n    )\n    return ConnectionRead.from_model(conn)\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/connections\",\n    response_model=list[ConnectionRead],\n    summary=\"List connections\",\n)\ndef list_connections(\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],\n    type: str | None = None,\n    env: str | None = None,\n) -> list[ConnectionRead]:\n    conns = svc.list_connections(db, conn_type=type, env=env)\n    return [ConnectionRead.from_model(c) for c in conns]\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/connections/{connection_id}\",\n    response_model=ConnectionRead,\n    summary=\"Get a connection\",\n)\ndef get_connection(\n    connection_id: uuid.UUID,\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],\n) -> ConnectionRead:\n    return ConnectionRead.from_model(svc.get_connection(db, connection_id))\n\n\n@router.patch(\n    \"/connections/{connection_id}\",\n    response_model=ConnectionRead,\n    summary=\"Update a connection\",\n)\ndef update_connection(\n    connection_id: uuid.UUID,\n    payload: ConnectionUpdate,\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],\n    secret_store: Annotated[SecretStore, Depends(get_secret_store)],\n) -> ConnectionRead:\n    conn = svc.update_connection(\n        db,\n        connection_id,\n        name=payload.name,\n        config=payload.config,\n        secret=payload.secret,\n        secret_store=secret_store,\n    )\n    return ConnectionRead.from_model(conn)\n\n\n@router.delete(\n    \"/connections/{connection_id}\",\n    status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT,\n    summary=\"Delete a connection\",\n)\ndef delete_connection(\n    connection_id: uuid.UUID,\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],\n) -> None:\n    svc.delete_connection(db, connection_id)\n\n\n@router.post(\n    \"/connections/{connection_id}/test\",\n    response_model=ConnectionTestResult,\n    summary=\"Test live connectivity for a connection\",\n)\ndef test_connection(\n    connection_id: uuid.UUID,\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],\n    secret_store: Annotated[SecretStore, Depends(get_secret_store)],\n) -> ConnectionTestResult:\n    # sync def → runs in a threadpool; the datasource connect is blocking.\n    svc.test_connection(db, connection_id, secret_store=secret_store)\n    return ConnectionTestResult(ok=True)\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/backend/tests/api/test_connections.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Connection endpoint tests against a real Postgres (db_session) via TestClient.\n\nget_db + get_secret_store are overridden to a shared test session and an\nin-memory store; the connectivity adapter is monkeypatched so /test needs no\nlive warehouse. Auth runs in dev-bypass mode (conftest), which upserts the dev\nuser into the same session for the created_by FK. Skips without\nTEST_DATABASE_URL.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport uuid\nfrom collections.abc import Iterator\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi import HTTPException\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\nfrom sqlalchemy import select\n\nfrom backend.app.core.auth import get_current_user\nfrom backend.app.core.secrets import get_secret_store\nfrom backend.app.db.models import Connection\nfrom backend.app.db.session import get_db\nfrom backend.app.main import app\nfrom backend.app.services import connection_service as svc\n\n_SF_CONFIG = {\n    \"account\": \"ab12345.eu-west-1\",\n    \"user\": \"svc_dataq\",\n    \"database\": \"ANALYTICS\",\n    \"schema\": \"FINANCE\",\n    \"warehouse\": \"WH_DQ\",\n    \"role\": \"DQ_ROLE\",\n}\n\n\nclass FakeStore:\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self.data: dict[str, str] = {}\n\n    def get(self, name: str) -> str:\n        return self.data[name]\n\n    def set(self, name: str, value: str) -> None:\n        self.data[name] = value\n\n\nclass _PassAdapter:\n    def validate_config(self, raw: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:\n        return None\n\n    def test(self, raw: dict[str, Any], secret: str) -> None:\n        return None\n\n\nclass _FailAdapter(_PassAdapter):\n    def test(self, raw: dict[str, Any], secret: str) -> None:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"warehouse unreachable\")\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(db_session: Any) -> Iterator[tuple[TestClient, FakeStore]]:\n    store = FakeStore()\n    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db_session\n    app.dependency_overrides[get_secret_store] = lambda: store\n    try:\n        yield TestClient(app), store\n    finally:\n        app.dependency_overrides.clear()\n\n\ndef _create_payload(**overrides: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    payload: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"name\": \"finance-dev\",\n        \"type\": \"snowflake\",\n        \"env\": \"dev\",\n        \"config\": dict(_SF_CONFIG),\n        \"secret\": \"p@ss\",\n    }\n    payload.update(overrides)\n    return payload\n\n\n# ───────────────────────── create ──────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef test_create_returns_201_and_hides_secret(\n    client: tuple[TestClient, FakeStore], db_session: Any\n) -> None:\n    api, store = client\n    resp = api.post(\"/api/v1/connections\", json=_create_payload())\n\n    assert resp.status_code == 201\n    body = resp.json()\n    assert body[\"type\"] == \"snowflake\"\n    assert body[\"has_secret\"] is True\n    # secret material must never appear in the response\n    assert \"secret\" not in body\n    assert \"secret_ref\" not in body\n    # persisted + written through to the store\n    conn = db_session.get(Connection, uuid.UUID(body[\"id\"]))\n    assert conn is not None\n    assert store.data[f\"conn-{conn.id}\"] == \"p@ss\"\n\n\ndef test_create_unknown_type_returns_422(client: tuple[TestClient, FakeStore]) -> None:\n    api, _ = client\n    resp = api.post(\"/api/v1/connections\", json=_create_payload(type=\"mssql\"))\n    assert resp.status_code == 422\n    assert resp.json()[\"error\"][\"code\"] == \"connection_config_invalid\"\n\n\ndef test_create_invalid_config_returns_422(client: tuple[TestClient, FakeStore]) -> None:\n    api, _ = client\n    bad = {k: v for k, v in _SF_CONFIG.items() if k != \"account\"}\n    resp = api.post(\"/api/v1/connections\", json=_create_payload(config=bad))\n    assert resp.status_code == 422\n\n\n# ───────────────────────── read / list ─────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef test_list_returns_created(client: tuple[TestClient, FakeStore]) -> None:\n    api, _ = client\n    api.post(\"/api/v1/connections\", json=_create_payload(name=\"a\"))\n    api.post(\"/api/v1/connections\", json=_create_payload(name=\"b\", env=\"qa\"))\n\n    all_conns = api.get(\"/api/v1/connections\").json()\n    assert {c[\"name\"] for c in all_conns} == {\"a\", \"b\"}\n    qa = api.get(\"/api/v1/connections\", params={\"env\": \"qa\"}).json()\n    assert [c[\"name\"] for c in qa] == [\"b\"]\n\n\ndef test_get_returns_connection(client: tuple[TestClient, FakeStore]) -> None:\n    api, _ = client\n    cid = api.post(\"/api/v1/connections\", json=_create_payload()).json()[\"id\"]\n    resp = api.get(f\"/api/v1/connections/{cid}\")\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    assert resp.json()[\"id\"] == cid\n\n\ndef test_get_unknown_returns_404(client: tuple[TestClient, FakeStore]) -> None:\n    api, _ = client\n    resp = api.get(f\"/api/v1/connections/{uuid.uuid4()}\")\n    assert resp.status_code == 404\n    assert resp.json()[\"error\"][\"code\"] == \"connection_not_found\"\n\n\n# ───────────────────────── update / delete ─────────────────────────\n\n\ndef test_patch_updates_name(client: tuple[TestClient, FakeStore]) -> None:\n    api, _ = client\n    cid = api.post(\"/api/v1/connections\", json=_create_payload()).json()[\"id\"]\n    resp = api.patch(f\"/api/v1/connections/{cid}\", json={\"name\": \"renamed\"})\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    assert resp.json()[\"name\"] == \"renamed\"\n\n\ndef test_delete_returns_204_then_404(client: tuple[TestClient, FakeStore]) -> None:\n    api, _ = client\n    cid = api.post(\"/api/v1/connections\", json=_create_payload()).json()[\"id\"]\n    assert api.delete(f\"/api/v1/connections/{cid}\").status_code == 204\n    assert api.get(f\"/api/v1/connections/{cid}\").status_code == 404\n\n\n# ───────────────────────── test connectivity ───────────────────────\n\n\ndef test_test_endpoint_ok(\n    client: tuple[TestClient, FakeStore], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch\n) -> None:\n    api, _ = client\n    cid = api.post(\"/api/v1/connections\", json=_create_payload()).json()[\"id\"]\n    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, \"get_connection_adapter\", lambda t: _PassAdapter())\n    resp = api.post(f\"/api/v1/connections/{cid}/test\")\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    assert resp.json() == {\"ok\": True}\n\n\ndef test_test_endpoint_failure_returns_502(\n    client: tuple[TestClient, FakeStore], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch\n) -> None:\n    api, _ = client\n    cid = api.post(\"/api/v1/connections\", json=_create_payload()).json()[\"id\"]\n    monkeypatch.setattr(svc, \"get_connection_adapter\", lambda t: _FailAdapter())\n    resp = api.post(f\"/api/v1/connections/{cid}/test\")\n    assert resp.status_code == 502\n    assert resp.json()[\"error\"][\"code\"] == \"connection_test_failed\"\n\n\n# ───────────────────────── auth gating ─────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef test_create_requires_auth(db_session: Any) -> None:\n    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db_session\n    app.dependency_overrides[get_secret_store] = lambda: FakeStore()\n\n    def _reject() -> None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail=\"unauthorized\")\n\n    app.dependency_overrides[get_current_user] = _reject\n    try:\n        resp = TestClient(app).post(\"/api/v1/connections\", json=_create_payload())\n        assert resp.status_code == 401\n        assert db_session.scalars(select(Connection)).all() == []\n    finally:\n        app.dependency_overrides.clear()\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "gh pr create --base main --head feature/connection-crud-snowflake \\\n  --title \"feat: Snowflake connection CRUD + test endpoint (PR 5)\" \\\n  --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## What\n\nPR 5 — the connection-manager foundation, with **Snowflake** as the reference datasource type and a `POST /test` connectivity check. This is the template PRs 6-8 (ADF, ADLS/S3, Unity Catalog) clone. Starts **Week 2** (Connection manager — backend).\n\nNo Alembic migration: the `connections` model already carries every column.\n\n## The seam (so PRs 6-8 are pure additions)\n\nIntroduces a per-type **`ConnectionAdapter`** Protocol next to `CheckRunner`, registered in `datasources/registry.py`. The CRUD service dispatches by `connection.type` and **never branches on it** — adding a datasource type is one registry entry + its adapter (no service/API churn). Consistent with the repo's existing seams and [ADR 0011](docs/adr/0011-extensibility-seams-for-deferred-integrations.md).\n\n## Commits (one functionality each)\n\n1. `feat(secrets)` — `SecretStore.set` write capability (AKV `set_secret`; Env dev-only)\n2. `feat(datasources)` — `ConnectionAdapter` seam + `SnowflakeConnectionAdapter` (validate + `SELECT 1` test) + registry\n3. `feat(services)` — `connection_service` CRUD + `test_connection` (typed `DataQError`s; 100% covered)\n4. `feat(api)` — `/api/v1/connections` router + main wiring\n\n## Security / design notes\n\n- Credentials are **write-through** to the `SecretStore` under a row-derived `conn-<uuid>` ref; only the ref is persisted. Responses expose `has_secret: bool` — **never** the secret or its ref.\n- Reuses the existing `get_current_user` boundary ([ADR 0010](docs/adr/0010-provider-agnostic-infrastructure-seams.md)) — no MSAL claims in route/service code.\n- `/test` is a sync `def` → blocking Snowflake connect runs in a threadpool, not the event loop.\n\n## Testing\n\n- **45 new tests** (secrets write path, adapter + registry, service CRUD against real Postgres, API endpoints via TestClient). Full suite: **120 passed, 1 skipped, ~94% coverage**.\n- Local gate clean: ruff, black, `mypy backend/app/`, bandit, full pre-commit (gitleaks).\n- **Manual smoke** against live uvicorn + Postgres: create (secret not leaked) → list → get → patch → test (502, no live warehouse) → invalid-type (422) → delete (204) → get-after-delete (404), all as expected.\n\n## Follow-ups (later Week 2 PRs, per plan)\n- PR 6: ADF connection CRUD + `(type, env)` uniqueness guard (#72, now documented)\n- PR 9: connection re-auth endpoint + `secret_ref` nullability review\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -2",
  "description": "Open PR 5"
}
```

```
diff --git a/backend/app/api/v1/connections.py b/backend/app/api/v1/connections.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..8cca8c4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/api/v1/connections.py
@@ -0,0 +1,174 @@
+"""Connection CRUD + connectivity-test endpoints.
+
+Thin HTTP layer over `connection_service`: validates request shapes, wires the
+current user + db session + secret store, and maps models onto responses. All
+business logic (validation dispatch, secret write-through, connectivity probe)
+lives in the service. Responses never carry secret material — only `has_secret`.
+
+The `/test` route is a sync ``def`` so FastAPI runs it in a worker thread; the
+Snowflake connect is blocking and must not stall the event loop.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import uuid
+from typing import Annotated, Any
+
+from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, status
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
+from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
+
+from backend.app.core.auth import get_current_user
+from backend.app.core.secrets import SecretStore, get_secret_store
+from backend.app.db.models import Connection, User
+from backend.app.db.session import get_db
+from backend.app.services import connection_service as svc
+
+router = APIRouter(tags=["connections"])
+
+
+class ConnectionCreate(BaseModel):
+    name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=128)
+    type: str
+    env: str
+    config: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
+    secret: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Credential; write-only, never returned")
+
+
+class ConnectionUpdate(BaseModel):
+    name: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=128)
+    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None
+    secret: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Rotate the credential; write-only")
+
+
+class ConnectionRead(BaseModel):
+    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)
+
+    id: uuid.UUID
+    name: str
+    type: str
+    env: str
+    config: dict[str, Any]
+    has_secret: bool
+    created_by: uuid.UUID
+
+    @classmethod
+    def from_model(cls, conn: Connection) -> ConnectionRead:
+        return cls(
+            id=conn.id,
+            name=conn.name,
+            type=conn.type,
+            env=conn.env,
+            config=dict(conn.config),
+            has_secret=conn.secret_ref is not None,
+            created_by=conn.created_by,
+        )
+
+
+class ConnectionTestResult(BaseModel):
+    ok: bool
+
+
+@router.post(
+    "/connections",
+    response_model=ConnectionRead,
+    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
+    summary="Create a connection",
+)
+def create_connection(
+    payload: ConnectionCreate,
+    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],
+    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],
+    secret_store: Annotated[SecretStore, Depends(get_secret_store)],
+) -> ConnectionRead:
+    conn = svc.create_connection(
+        db,
+        name=payload.name,
+        conn_type=payload.type,
+        env=payload.env,
+        config=payload.config,
+        secret=payload.secret,
+        created_by=current_user.id,
+        secret_store=secret_store,
+    )
+    return ConnectionRead.from_model(conn)
+
+
+@router.get(
+    "/connections",
+    response_model=list[ConnectionRead],
+    summary="List connections",
+)
+def list_connections(
+    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],
+    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],
+    type: str | None = None,
+    env: str | None = None,
+) -> list[ConnectionRead]:
+    conns = svc.list_connections(db, conn_type=type, env=env)
+    return [ConnectionRead.from_model(c) for c in conns]
+
+
+@router.get(
+    "/connections/{connection_id}",
+    response_model=ConnectionRead,
+    summary="Get a connection",
+)
+def get_connection(
+    connection_id: uuid.UUID,
+    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],
+    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],
+) -> ConnectionRead:
+    return ConnectionRead.from_model(svc.get_connection(db, connection_id))
+
+
+@router.patch(
+    "/connections/{connection_id}",
+    response_model=ConnectionRead,
+    summary="Update a connection",
+)
+def update_connection(
+    connection_id: uuid.UUID,
+    payload: ConnectionUpdate,
+    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],
+    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],
+    secret_store: Annotated[SecretStore, Depends(get_secret_store)],
+) -> ConnectionRead:
+    conn = svc.update_connection(
+        db,
+        connection_id,
+        name=payload.name,
+        config=payload.config,
+        secret=payload.secret,
+        secret_store=secret_store,
+    )
+    return ConnectionRead.from_model(conn)
+
+
+@router.delete(
+    "/connections/{connection_id}",
+    status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT,
+    summary="Delete a connection",
+)
+def delete_connection(
+    connection_id: uuid.UUID,
+    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],
+    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],
+) -> None:
+    svc.delete_connection(db, connection_id)
+
+
+@router.post(
+    "/connections/{connection_id}/test",
+    response_model=ConnectionTestResult,
+    summary="Test live connectivity for a connection",
+)
+def test_connection(
+    connection_id: uuid.UUID,
+    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],
+    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],
+    secret_store: Annotated[SecretStore, Depends(get_secret_store)],
+) -> ConnectionTestResult:
+    # sync def → runs in a threadpool; the datasource connect is blocking.
+    svc.test_connection(db, connection_id, secret_store=secret_store)
+    return ConnectionTestResult(ok=True)
diff --git a/backend/app/core/secrets.py b/backend/app/core/secrets.py
index 0958e65..fa70885 100644
--- a/backend/app/core/secrets.py
+++ b/backend/app/core/secrets.py
@@ -39,10 +39,16 @@ class SecretNotFoundError(Exception):
     """Raised when the requested secret is missing or unreadable."""
 
 
+class SecretWriteError(Exception):
+    """Raised when a secret cannot be written to the backing store."""
+
+
 @runtime_checkable
 class SecretStore(Protocol):
     def get(self, name: str) -> str: ...
 
+    def set(self, name: str, value: str) -> None: ...
+
 
 def _env_key(name: str) -> str:
     return f"{ENV_PREFIX}{name.upper().replace('-', '_')}"
@@ -58,6 +64,14 @@ class EnvSecretStore:
             raise SecretNotFoundError(f"Env secret {key!r} not set (mapped from name={name!r})")
         return value
 
+    def set(self, name: str, value: str) -> None:
+        """Write into the process env. Dev only — NOT persisted across restarts.
+
+        Lets connection-CRUD exercise the write-through path locally without an
+        Azure tenant. Production uses AzureKeyVaultStore, which persists.
+        """
+        os.environ[_env_key(name)] = value
+
 
 class AzureKeyVaultStore:
     """Resolves secrets from Azure Key Vault via DefaultAzureCredential."""
@@ -93,6 +107,14 @@ class AzureKeyVaultStore:
             raise SecretNotFoundError(f"Key Vault secret {name!r} has no value")
         return str(value)
 
+    def set(self, name: str, value: str) -> None:
+        try:
+            self._client_lazy().set_secret(name, value)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            raise SecretWriteError(
+                f"Key Vault secret {name!r} at {self._vault_url}: {exc}"
+            ) from exc
+
 
 _store_singleton: SecretStore | None = None
 _store_lock = threading.Lock()
diff --git a/backend/app/datasources/base.py b/backend/app/datasources/base.py
index c5d9103..3a25081 100644
--- a/backend/app/datasources/base.py
+++ b/backend/app/datasources/base.py
@@ -18,6 +18,8 @@ from __future__ import annotations
 from dataclasses import dataclass
 from typing import Any, Protocol, runtime_checkable
 
+from pydantic import BaseModel
+
 
 @dataclass(frozen=True)
 class CheckSpec:
@@ -69,3 +71,25 @@ class CheckRunner(Protocol):
         schema: str | None,
         checks: list[CheckSpec],
     ) -> SuiteOutcome: ...
+
+
+@runtime_checkable
+class ConnectionAdapter(Protocol):
+    """Per-datasource-type connection behaviour: config validation + live test.
+
+    The two things that vary across connection types (Snowflake now; ADF, ADLS,
+    S3, Unity Catalog next) behind one interface, so connection-CRUD service code
+    dispatches by ``connection.type`` and never branches on it. Each adapter owns
+    its own pydantic config model; both methods take the raw config dict so the
+    adapter is the single source of truth for that type's shape.
+
+    `validate_config` parses + validates a stored/incoming config (raising
+    pydantic ``ValidationError`` on bad input) and returns the normalised model.
+    `test` resolves connectivity against the live datasource using the config +
+    its secret, raising on failure. Adapters never touch the DB or SecretStore —
+    the caller resolves the secret and hands it in.
+    """
+
+    def validate_config(self, raw: dict[str, Any]) -> BaseModel: ...
+
+    def test(self, raw: dict[str, Any], secret: str) -> None: ...
diff --git a/backend/app/datasources/registry.py b/backend/app/datasources/registry.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..975dc6d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/datasources/registry.py
@@ -0,0 +1,30 @@
+"""Connection-type → adapter registry.
+
+The single place that maps a ``Connection.type`` to its `ConnectionAdapter`.
+Adding a datasource type (ADF, ADLS Gen2, S3, Unity Catalog — Weeks 2-3) is a
+one-line entry here plus the adapter itself; connection-CRUD service code stays
+untouched, dispatching through `get_connection_adapter`.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from backend.app.datasources.base import ConnectionAdapter
+from backend.app.datasources.snowflake import SnowflakeConnectionAdapter
+
+
+class UnsupportedConnectionTypeError(ValueError):
+    """Raised when no adapter is registered for a connection type."""
+
+
+_ADAPTERS: dict[str, ConnectionAdapter] = {
+    "snowflake": SnowflakeConnectionAdapter(),
+}
+
+
+def get_connection_adapter(conn_type: str) -> ConnectionAdapter:
+    adapter = _ADAPTERS.get(conn_type)
+    if adapter is None:
+        raise UnsupportedConnectionTypeError(
+            f"No connection adapter registered for type {conn_type!r}"
+        )
+    return adapter
diff --git a/backend/app/datasources/snowflake.py b/backend/app/datasources/snowflake.py
index b592993..1ff32f0 100644
--- a/backend/app/datasources/snowflake.py
+++ b/backend/app/datasources/snowflake.py
@@ -185,3 +185,37 @@ def build_snowflake_runner(
     sf_config = SnowflakeConfig.model_validate(config)
     password = secret_store.get(secret_ref)
     return SnowflakeCheckRunner(sf_config, password)
+
+
+# Snowflake connector timeouts (seconds) for the connectivity test — fail fast
+# rather than hanging the request thread on an unreachable account.
+_TEST_LOGIN_TIMEOUT = 10
+_TEST_NETWORK_TIMEOUT = 10
+
+
+class SnowflakeConnectionAdapter:
+    """`ConnectionAdapter` for Snowflake — config validation + a SELECT 1 test."""
+
+    def validate_config(self, raw: dict[str, Any]) -> SnowflakeConfig:
+        return SnowflakeConfig.model_validate(raw)
+
+    def test(self, raw: dict[str, Any], secret: str) -> None:
+        """Open a connection and run ``SELECT 1``; raise on any failure.
+
+        Deliberately GX-free — a lightweight connectivity probe, not a suite run.
+        """
+        from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
+
+        config = self.validate_config(raw)
+        engine = create_engine(
+            build_connection_string(config, secret),
+            connect_args={
+                "login_timeout": _TEST_LOGIN_TIMEOUT,
+                "network_timeout": _TEST_NETWORK_TIMEOUT,
+            },
+        )
+        try:
+            with engine.connect() as conn:
+                conn.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
+        finally:
+            engine.dispose()

diff --git a/backend/app/services/connection_service.py b/backend/app/services/connection_service.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b88d3c0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/services/connection_service.py
@@ -0,0 +1,202 @@
+"""Connection CRUD + connectivity test, datasource-type-agnostic.
+
+Drives the `connections` table and dispatches type-specific behaviour through
+the `ConnectionAdapter` registry — so this layer never branches on
+``connection.type``. Credentials are written through the `SecretStore`
+(`set`) and only ever referenced by `Connection.secret_ref`; the plaintext
+secret is never stored on the row or logged.
+
+FastAPI-free by design (like `run_service`): takes a `Session` + `SecretStore`,
+returns ORM models, raises `DataQError` subclasses. The API layer owns
+request/response shapes and dependency wiring.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import uuid
+from typing import Any
+
+from pydantic import ValidationError
+from sqlalchemy import select
+from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
+
+from backend.app.core.errors import DataQError
+from backend.app.core.logging import get_logger
+from backend.app.core.secrets import SecretNotFoundError, SecretStore
+from backend.app.datasources.registry import (
+    UnsupportedConnectionTypeError,
+    get_connection_adapter,
+)
+from backend.app.db.models import Connection
+
+log = get_logger(__name__)
+
+
+class ConnectionNotFoundError(DataQError):
+    status_code = 404
+    code = "connection_not_found"
+
+
+class ConnectionConfigInvalidError(DataQError):
+    status_code = 422
+    code = "connection_config_invalid"
+
+
+class ConnectionTestFailedError(DataQError):
+    status_code = 502
+    code = "connection_test_failed"
+
+
+def _validated_config(conn_type: str, config: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
+    """Reject an unknown type or a config that fails its adapter's schema."""
+    try:
+        adapter = get_connection_adapter(conn_type)
+    except UnsupportedConnectionTypeError as exc:
+        raise ConnectionConfigInvalidError(str(exc), detail={"type": conn_type}) from exc
+    try:
+        adapter.validate_config(config)
+    except ValidationError as exc:
+        raise ConnectionConfigInvalidError(
+            f"Invalid config for {conn_type!r} connection",
+            detail={"errors": exc.errors()},
+        ) from exc
+
+
+def create_connection(
+    session: Session,
+    *,
+    name: str,
+    conn_type: str,
+    env: str,
+    config: dict[str, Any],
+    secret: str | None,
+    created_by: uuid.UUID,
+    secret_store: SecretStore,
+) -> Connection:
+    """Validate, persist, and (if a secret is given) write its credential.
+
+    The secret_ref is derived from the row's own id (``conn-<uuid>``) — unique
+    and safe as a Key Vault secret name. The credential is written through the
+    store; only the ref is persisted on the row.
+    """
+    _validated_config(conn_type, config)
+
+    conn = Connection(
+        name=name,
+        type=conn_type,
+        env=env,
+        config=config,
+        secret_ref=None,
+        created_by=created_by,
+    )
+    session.add(conn)
+    session.flush()  # assign conn.id for the secret_ref
+
+    if secret is not None:
+        secret_ref = f"conn-{conn.id}"
+        secret_store.set(secret_ref, secret)
+        conn.secret_ref = secret_ref
+
+    session.commit()
+    session.refresh(conn)
+    log.info("connection_created", connection_id=str(conn.id), type=conn_type, env=env)
+    return conn
+
+
+def list_connections(
+    session: Session,
+    *,
+    conn_type: str | None = None,
+    env: str | None = None,
+) -> list[Connection]:
+    stmt = select(Connection).order_by(Connection.created_at.desc())
+    if conn_type is not None:
+        stmt = stmt.where(Connection.type == conn_type)
+    if env is not None:
+        stmt = stmt.where(Connection.env == env)
+    return list(session.scalars(stmt))
+
+
+def get_connection(session: Session, connection_id: uuid.UUID) -> Connection:
+    conn = session.get(Connection, connection_id)
+    if conn is None:
+        raise ConnectionNotFoundError(
+            "connection not found", detail={"connection_id": str(connection_id)}
+        )
+    return conn
+
+
+def update_connection(
+    session: Session,
+    connection_id: uuid.UUID,
+    *,
+    name: str | None = None,
+    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
+    secret: str | None = None,
+    secret_store: SecretStore,
+) -> Connection:
+    """Partial update of name / config / secret. Type and env are immutable."""
+    conn = get_connection(session, connection_id)
+
+    if config is not None:
+        _validated_config(conn.type, config)
+        conn.config = config
+    if name is not None:
+        conn.name = name
+    if secret is not None:
+        secret_ref = conn.secret_ref or f"conn-{conn.id}"
+        secret_store.set(secret_ref, secret)
+        conn.secret_ref = secret_ref
+
+    session.commit()
+    session.refresh(conn)
+    log.info("connection_updated", connection_id=str(conn.id))
+    return conn
+
+
+def delete_connection(session: Session, connection_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
+    conn = get_connection(session, connection_id)
+    session.delete(conn)
+    session.commit()
+    log.info("connection_deleted", connection_id=str(connection_id))
+
+
+def test_connection(
+    session: Session,
+    connection_id: uuid.UUID,
+    *,
+    secret_store: SecretStore,
+) -> None:
+    """Resolve the connection's secret and probe live connectivity.
+
+    Raises `ConnectionTestFailedError` (502) on missing credentials or any
+    adapter-reported connectivity failure.
+    """
+    conn = get_connection(session, connection_id)
+    adapter = get_connection_adapter(conn.type)
+
+    if not conn.secret_ref:
+        raise ConnectionTestFailedError(
+            "connection has no stored credential to test with",
+            detail={"connection_id": str(connection_id)},
+        )
+    try:
+        secret = secret_store.get(conn.secret_ref)
+    except SecretNotFoundError as exc:
+        raise ConnectionTestFailedError(
+            "credential could not be resolved", detail={"connection_id": str(connection_id)}
+        ) from exc
+
+    try:
+        adapter.test(dict(conn.config), secret)
+    except Exception as exc:
+        log.warning(
+            "connection_test_failed",
+            connection_id=str(connection_id),
+            error_type=type(exc).__name__,
+        )
+        raise ConnectionTestFailedError(
+            f"connection test failed: {exc}", detail={"connection_id": str(connection_id)}
+        ) from exc
+
+    log.info("connection_test_succeeded", connection_id=str(connection_id))
```

```
from collections.abc import Iterator

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from backend.app.core.config import get_settings


def _build_engine() -> Engine:
    settings = get_settings()
    return create_engine(settings.database_url, pool_pre_ping=True, future=True)


engine: Engine = _build_engine()
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False, autocommit=False, future=True)


def get_session() -> Session:
    return SessionLocal()


def get_db() -> Iterator[Session]:
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()
```

```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ\nSHA=$(git rev-parse HEAD)\necho \"head sha: $SHA\"\nREPO=TheurgicDuke771/DataQ\nPR=85\n\n# Finding 1 — secret/DSN leak via test error message echoed to client\ngh api repos/$REPO/pulls/$PR/comments -X POST \\\n  -f commit_id=\"$SHA\" -f path=\"backend/app/services/connection_service.py\" -F line=199 -f side=RIGHT \\\n  -f body='**[review] Possible credential/DSN leak in the client-facing error.** The adapter exception is deliberately kept out of the logs (line 194-198 logs only `error_type`) — but the same `exc` is interpolated verbatim into the error returned to the client: `f\"connection test failed: {exc}\"`. A SQLAlchemy/snowflake-connector failure can carry DSN fragments (account/user, and in some cases the URL) into `str(exc)`, which then lands in the API response. Recommend a generic client message (e.g. `\"connection test failed\"`) and keep any detail server-side only, consistent with the PII-redaction discipline (CLAUDE.md §10).' \\\n  >/dev/null && echo \"posted finding 1\"\n\n# Finding 2 — duplicate (name, env) -> unhandled IntegrityError -> 500\ngh api repos/$REPO/pulls/$PR/comments -X POST \\\n  -f commit_id=\"$SHA\" -f path=\"backend/app/services/connection_service.py\" -F line=100 -f side=RIGHT \\\n  -f body='**[review] Duplicate `(name, env)` returns 500 instead of a clean 4xx.** The model has `UniqueConstraint(\"name\", \"env\")` (models.py:74). Creating a second connection with the same name+env (an ordinary user action / retry) raises `IntegrityError` at `flush()`/`commit()` here, which is unhandled → the generic 500 `internal_error` envelope. Recommend catching `IntegrityError` and raising a typed `DataQError` (409 conflict) so the client gets an actionable response. No test covers this path yet.' \\\n  >/dev/null && echo \"posted finding 2\"\n\n# Finding 3 — env not validated against ENVS -> CHECK violation -> 500\ngh api repos/$REPO/pulls/$PR/comments -X POST \\\n  -f commit_id=\"$SHA\" -f path=\"backend/app/api/v1/connections.py\" -F line=33 -f side=RIGHT \\\n  -f body='**[review] `env` is unvalidated, so a bad value 500s.** `env: str` is free-text here, and the service passes it straight through to the row. The DB has `CHECK (env IN ('\"'\"'dev'\"'\"','\"'\"'qa'\"'\"','\"'\"'uat'\"'\"','\"'\"'prod'\"'\"'))` (models.py:73), so `env=\"staging\"` raises a CHECK violation at flush → unhandled → 500. (`type` is already gated to 422 via the adapter lookup; `env` has no such guard.) Recommend validating `env` against `ENVS` at the schema or service layer and raising `ConnectionConfigInvalidError` (422).' \\\n  >/dev/null && echo \"posted finding 3\"",
  "description": "Post 3 inline review comments to PR 85"
}
```

> AGENT

Pushed the assert fix (481e83d). Now — to your question: **"Are we filing the issues?"**

My read on what the working agreement actually requires here:

**The 3 review findings (409 / 422 / leak) do *not* need issues.** The rule *"defects → GitHub issue, never silent fixes, fixing PR includes `Fixes #N`"* targets defects in **already-merged** code — where a silent fix would hide a regression in `main`. These three were bugs in **PR 5's own un-merged code**, caught by pre-merge self-review. The convention for *that* is the one in my memory: *"run /review before merge; findings become PR comments, never silent fixes."* I did exactly that — posted them as inline PR comments, fixed them in the same PR, and replied confirming. Filing issues to immediately close them in the same PR would be ceremony with no audit value.

**But there are 2 things I deliberately deferred that *are* worth tracking issues** — known gaps we're consciously punting, which is precisely what issues are for:

1. **`SecretWriteError` → 500, not 502.** If the Key Vault write fails during create/update, it surfaces as a generic `internal_error` 500 rather than a clean `502`. Infra-level, lower priority — I didn't fold it into PR 5.
2. **`EnvSecretStore.set` is per-process.** A secret written by the API process isn't visible to the **Celery worker** process (separate `os.environ`). Dev-only (prod uses Key Vault), but it *will* bite once connection-driven runs land — the worker won't resolve a connection's credential. Worth a tracking issue so it's not a surprise in PR 5's follow-ons.

Let me confirm CI is clean, then settle the issues question:

```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ\nISSUE1=$(gh issue create --title \"EnvSecretStore.set is per-process — Celery worker can't resolve API-written secrets\" \\\n  --label \"bug\" \\\n  --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Context\n\nSurfaced during [PR #85](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/85) (PR 5 — Snowflake connection CRUD). `SecretStore.set` was added so connection-CRUD can write credentials through the store ([ADR 0010] auth/secrets boundary).\n\n`EnvSecretStore.set` (the **dev-only** backend) writes to the API process's `os.environ`:\n\n```python\ndef set(self, name: str, value: str) -> None:\n    os.environ[_env_key(name)] = value  # dev only, not persisted\n```\n\n## Problem\n\nA secret written by the **API process** is **not visible to the Celery worker process** — they have separate `os.environ`. Today this is harmless because:\n- Production uses `AzureKeyVaultStore` (shared, persistent) — unaffected.\n- The probe path uses `probe_snowflake_secret_ref`, a pre-set `KV_SECRET_*` env present in both processes.\n\nBut it **will break** once a **user-created connection's credential feeds `run_suite`** (the worker resolves `connection.secret_ref` via `get_secret_store().get(...)`). In a dev/compose multi-process setup, the worker's `EnvSecretStore` won't have the API-written secret → `SecretNotFoundError` → run fails.\n\n## Affected\n\nConnection-driven worker runs — the PR 5 follow-ons (flat-file / UC run paths in Week 5 that build a runner from a stored connection).\n\n## Options\n\n- Document that any multi-process dev setup must use `AzureKeyVaultStore` (or a shared secret backend), not `EnvSecretStore`.\n- Or add a shared dev-only backend (file/Redis-backed) so `set` in one process is visible to others.\n- Decide before connection-driven runs land.\n\n## Acceptance\n- [ ] Decision recorded (doc note or shared dev backend).\n- [ ] Worker-side secret resolution verified for a user-created connection in a multi-process dev run.\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -1)\necho \"filed: $ISSUE1\"\n\nISSUE2=$(gh issue create --title \"Map SecretStore write failure (SecretWriteError) to 502 in connection create/update\" \\\n  --label \"enhancement\" \\\n  --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Context\n\nSurfaced during [PR #85](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/85) (PR 5 — Snowflake connection CRUD).\n\nIn `connection_service.create_connection` / `update_connection`, the credential is written via `secret_store.set(...)`. If the backing store fails (e.g. Key Vault unreachable), `AzureKeyVaultStore.set` raises `SecretWriteError`, which is **not** a `DataQError` subclass → it propagates to the generic unhandled-exception handler → **500 `internal_error`**.\n\n## Wanted\n\nA Key Vault write failure is an upstream-dependency failure, so it should map to a clean **502** (consistent with `ConnectionTestFailedError`'s 502), not a generic 500.\n\n## Fix sketch\n\nCatch `SecretWriteError` around the `secret_store.set(...)` calls in create/update and raise a typed `DataQError` (502, e.g. `code=\"connection_secret_write_failed\"`).\n\n## Priority\n\nLow — infra failure path, not hit in normal operation. Tracked so the error-shape gap isn't forgotten in a later hardening pass.\n\n## Acceptance\n- [ ] `SecretWriteError` in create/update → 502 envelope (not 500).\n- [ ] Test covers the mapped failure.\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -1)\necho \"filed: $ISSUE2\"",
  "description": "Create the two tracking issues"
}
```
